# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 425–454

## Page 465 — IIIF Canvas #425

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0425.JP2` (5773 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0425.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0425.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

Treatise on kidney/bladder stones, sand, and gravel ('Von Stain sandt Vnd Jrieß'), featuring an illustration of an apothecary at a worktable with flasks, followed by recipes for woodlice powder, turpentine syrup, stone powders, pills, clysters, and draughts.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Von Stain sandt Vnd Jrieß** (`#xywh=0,0,5773,6853`): Hand-colored ink and watercolor illustration in the upper-left corner of the page depicting a bearded apothecary or physician with brown hair, dressed in a red doublet/coat with white cuffs, yellow-ochre breeches, and red stockings, leaning over a wooden table with glass alchemical flasks and vials (including a large round-bottomed flask filled with green liquid). Below the figure is a small landscape vignette with green grass, a brown bush/tree, and grey rocky ground.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0426.JP2` (5980 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0426.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0426.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,700

Continuation of remedies for stone, sand, and gravel, including laxative draughts and boluses, Jadanius's pills, herbal drinks, herbal baths, sand pills, a precious water distilled with snails, and ointments for urinary obstruction and colic.

## Page 467 — IIIF Canvas #427

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0427.JP2` (5759 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0427.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0427.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Remedies for kidney/bladder gravel and stones (including ivy, frog/elder, Venetian turpentine with five distilled waters, pigeon dung, and a multi-herb gravel bath), followed by a powder for gravel and phlegm, further recipes (woodlice, sal ammoniac, pigeon dung, garlic/onion trituration), an illustration of a urine flask/jug and chamber pot, and a section on obstructed urine (strangury/dysuria).

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Urine jug/flask and handled chamber pot on a wooden table** (`#xywh=0,0,5759,6853`): Centered in the right column above 'Den Verstopfften Vrin zu dreib'. On the left is a tapering blue-grey glass or ceramic pitcher/flask with a flared rim and bulbous base half-filled with golden-amber liquid, resting on a coiled yellow straw ring. On the right is a wide, low dark grey/black earthenware or pewter chamber pot with a single loop handle on the right side. Both vessels sit on a brown wooden table or shelf.

## Page 468 — IIIF Canvas #428

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0428.JP2` (6013 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0428.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0428.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,380

Continuation of diuretic remedies, a julep to drive urine, two herbal baths for urinary obstruction and gravel/stone pain, a lizard-based diuretic, a distilled water for gravel/stone and hernia carnosa, a strawberry-based colic/gravel remedy, an illustration of a urine matula/flask with a red urine symbol, and definitions/descriptions of dysuria, strangury, and diabetes.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Urine flask (matula) on a wooden table with red urine symbol (⊡)** (`#xywh=0,0,6013,6853`): Positioned in the middle of the right column next to the rubricated heading 'Disuria Strangúgria Hernia harn.' A cylindrical blue-grey glass flask (matula) with a flared rim, narrow neck, and bulbous base containing golden-amber liquid, resting on a coiled yellow straw ring. It sits on a brown wooden table/box with a blank white rectangular label panel on the front. To the upper right of the flask is a square with a central dot (⊡) drawn in red ink.

## Page 469 — IIIF Canvas #429

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0429.JP2` (5759 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0429.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0429.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,520

Continuation of the treatise on strangury and urinary obstruction: causes (swollen bladder neck, caruncles, stones, coagulated blood, ulcer matter, thick phlegm), involuntary urination from nerve paralysis vs. muscle weakness, diagnostic examination with a catheter, and external/internal treatments (oils, plasters, fumigations, baths, syrups, and stone-breaking remedies).

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0433.JP2` (5746 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0433.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0433.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

Remedies for urinary complaints (strangury/retention and nocturnal enuresis / inability to hold urine, marked with the square urine/bladder symbol), followed by a rubricated section on Gonorrhoea with a blank pasted rectangular label/box, describing its causes (weakness of retaining power vs. excessive expelling power, heat in the vasa spermatica), symptoms, and dietary/regimen precautions.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Blank pasted label under 'Gonorrchea'** (`#xywh=0,0,5746,6853`): A blank, cream-colored rectangular paper slip framed in red ink, positioned directly beneath the rubricated heading 'Gonorrchea' in the upper-middle left of the right column, with a small ink dot near the center.

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0434.JP2` (6034 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0434.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0434.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,380

Continuation of remedies for gonorrhoea and kidney/urinary ailments: kidney salves, cooling powders, turpentine and coral pills, distilled waters, balsams, purgative pills (with calomel/sweet mercury and fumitory), and an emollient topical application with sweet milk and quince mucilage.

## Page 476 — IIIF Canvas #435

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0435.JP2` (5772 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0435.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0435.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,520

Section on ulcers of the genitals/penis ('Von Geschwer des ♀'), detailing diet, purgation, warm cow's milk baths, soothing mucilage injections, and turpentine pills; followed in the right column by a watercolor anatomical diagram of the urinary bladder and urethra and a section distinguishing between bladder stones and urethral caruncles ('Von Undter schidt Der Griesen Zwischen dem Stain Und Carnuncula') using a bougie/catheter.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Anatomical diagram of the urinary bladder and urethra** (`#xywh=0,0,5772,6853`): A framed watercolor illustration in the middle of the right column depicting a rounded, orange-reddish urinary bladder with two sinuous/coiled ureters entering at the upper left and upper right, and a curved, tapering bladder neck/urethra descending downward to the right. Directly beneath the illustration is a blank rectangular label box framed in red ink.

## Page 477 — IIIF Canvas #436

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0436.JP2` (6019 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0436.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0436.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Remedies for urinary obstruction, urethral flesh/strictures using bougies (candela), and the beginning of a section on bladder ulcers ('Von Geschwer der Blasen') accompanied by a framed anatomical illustration of the urinary bladder.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Anatomical illustration of the urinary bladder** (`#xywh=0,0,6019,6853`): A hand-drawn anatomical illustration in red-brown ink and wash, framed in a square box in the upper-left portion of the right column beside the rubricated heading 'Von Geschwer der Blasen'. It depicts the urinary bladder as an ovoid sac with two sinuous ureters entering from the upper left and upper right sides, and a curved urethra extending downward and curving to the right.

Continuation of remedies for bladder ulcers, including emulsions, electuaries, herb sachets, Christof Maximilian's secret emulsion, a soothing bath ('Volgentes Bad'), injections, and pain-relieving ointments.

## Page 479 — IIIF Canvas #438

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0438.JP2` (6073 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0438.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0438.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,400

Further remedies and diagnostic distinctions between kidney, ureter, and bladder ulcers, followed by a section on bladder ulcers following gonorrhea and fleshy growths (carnositides) in the urinary canal, accompanied by a small anatomical diagram.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Anatomical diagram of a urethral caruncle / bladder neck obstruction** (`#xywh=0,0,6073,6853`): A small hand-drawn anatomical illustration in brown and reddish-brown ink and wash, positioned in the right column just above the rubricated heading 'Weilen aber nach denen Geschweren'. It shows an ovoid bladder-like sac on the right tapering into a narrow horizontal canal/tube extending to the left, illustrating a fleshy growth (carnositides) or obstruction in the urinary canal.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0439.JP2` (5765 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0439.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0439.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,450

## Page 482 — IIIF Canvas #441

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0441.JP2` (5793 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0441.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0441.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

A new treatise on ruptures (hernias) of the body, illustrated with a hand-coloured figure of a man wearing a hernia truss, classifying five types of hernias and true vs. incomplete ruptures, followed by four precious rupture plasters.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Anatomical figure of a standing man wearing a hernia truss** (`#xywh=0,0,5793,6853`): Hand-coloured pen-and-ink wash illustration in the upper-left area of the page depicting a standing, beardless man with shoulder-length curly brown hair, naked except for a red and yellow sash/truss tied around his waist and groin (hernia bandage/suspensory). His right hand rests on his chest while his left hand holds out the long trailing red-and-yellow band of the truss. He stands on a small patch of brownish-green ground.

### Upper Right & Full-Width Section

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | 482 | 482 *— Note: Boxed page number in red ink at top right* |
| 2 | Von den Brüen des Leibs. Wie | Of the Ruptures (Hernias) of the Body, How *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 3 | Solliche in der Zeit zu Churieren Vnd | the Same in Time to Cure and *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 4 | Zu hellfen Ist. | to Help. *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |

| 6 | nacher bruch. oder ruptur. werde mit mehr als zweigerley gefundn | afterwards calls rupture or hernia, is found in no more than two kinds: |
| 7 | das andere aber werde genant Hernia. es werde zwar ins gemein | the other, however, is named hernia; although in general |
| 8 | ach bruch genant. sind aber keine bruch. weilen nichts herauß der Verbroch | also called rupture, [they] are not ruptures, because nothing has broken out |

Single-column page describing types of hernias and their treatments: Hernia Ventosa (wind rupture) with carminative oils and cataplasms, Hernia Malacere saniose (soft/sanious hernia), and the beginning of Hernia Carnosa (fleshy hernia / sarcocele).

### Full Page

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Hernia Ventosa Windt Bruch ~ | Hernia Ventosa (Wind Rupture) *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 2 | 88 | 88 *— Note: Page number in top-right corner (488, with '4' faint or trimmed)* |
| 3 | dises ist ein auf blähung des Scroto, oder schlaue ein gar kleine hengen, wie ein oder beid | This is a swelling/inflation of the scrotum, or [in] the skin a very small hanging, as one or both |
| 4 | wärnß. dickhlichig. beiderlen befinden sich die Hernia Ventosa mit andern feichtig kheiten | testicles thickly. In both ways the hernia ventosa is found with other humors |
| 5 | beigleiht, mit kindz vermischt. wann die feuchte. gall. bluedt prodominiert. so han der | accompanied, mixed with wind. When the moist gall [or] blood predominates, then they have |
| 6 | die ansetzung ein schmerzlich apostem. u grosse Entzündung, so aber die melancolia sicht | the onset of a painful aposteme and great inflammation; if however melancholy is seen |

Two-column page continuing the discussion of Hernia Carnosa (with Emplastrum de ranis cum mercurio of Giovanni da Vigo), followed by Hernia Varicosa sanguinosa (varicocele), a section on groin swellings (Von den Triesten in Reichen), and concluding remedies for ruptures and Hernia aquosa (hydrocele), accompanied by a small oval red-ink diagram.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Unlabeled oval red-ink anatomical/hernia diagram** (`#xywh=0,0,6013,6853`): Small shaded oval/pear-shaped drawing in reddish-brown ink positioned to the left of the rubricated heading 'Volgen noch slüß mitl Vor die brüch' in the lower right column, depicting a swelling or scrotal/hernial sac.

## Page 501 — IIIF Canvas #446

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0446.JP2` (6044 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0446.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0446.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,380

Treatise on encysted tumors and swellings (Atheroma, Steatoma, Meliceris, Ganglion, Struma/Scrofula), including plaster recipes, an anatomical/surgical illustration of a man with multiple tumors, and surgical excision instructions.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**(Unlabeled anatomical/surgical illustration showing a man with tumors)** (`#xywh=0,0,6044,6853`): A colored pen-and-ink and watercolor illustration in the upper half of the right column. It depicts a nude male figure from the waist up, turned in three-quarter view to the left, with short blond hair. In his raised right hand he holds a tall dark blue-grey cylindrical cup or vessel. Several circular reddish-brown swellings or encysted tumors (illustrating atheroma, steatoma, meliceris, etc.) are drawn on his left cheek, the side of his neck, his left shoulder, a large one on his right chest/breast, and one on his right elbow.

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0447.JP2` (5776 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0447.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0447.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,580

Continuation of surgical excision and caustic treatment of encysted tumors, warning against operating on growths near the spine, followed by an illustration of a woman with a shoulder tumor and recipes for softening hard boils and apostemes.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**(Unlabeled anatomical/surgical illustration showing a woman with a shoulder tumor)** (`#xywh=0,0,5776,6853`): A colored pen-and-ink and watercolor illustration in the upper portion of the right column. It depicts a nude female figure from the chest up, facing forward with her head slightly turned to the left and her blonde hair swept back. Her right arm is raised with her hand open beside her face. On her left shoulder, a prominent raised circular reddish-brown swelling or tumor is depicted.

## Page 504 — IIIF Canvas #449

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0449.JP2` (5784 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0449.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0449.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,550

Illustrated treatise and recipes on treating burns and scalds caused by water, oil, gunpowder, or wax, including cooling salves, lime-water washes, and eye-protecting ointments.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Kitchen/laboratory scene illustrating scalding and burns** (`#xywh=0,0,5784,6853`): Coloured ink-and-wash illustration in the upper-left column depicting a woman in a blue bodice, white apron, and red skirt standing in a doorway with her right hand raised in alarm, looking toward a brick hearth where a large cauldron hangs over a blazing fire from a chain and wooden beam, with pots, a ladle, and spilled liquid on the hearth and jars and pans on a shelf above.

## Page 505 — IIIF Canvas #450

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0450.JP2` (6023 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0450.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0450.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Discourse on sympathetic healing and transplantation, recounting conversations with a Jesuit (Father Bernardin) and a Bishop defending sympathetic powder as a natural effect rather than a diabolical pact, followed by a definition of sympathetic healing using copper vitriol.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Sympathetic healing and transplantation through a tree** (`#xywh=0,0,6023,6853`): Coloured ink-and-wash illustration in the upper-left column depicting a woman in a white headdress, red skirt, and white apron holding the outstretched hands/arms of a horizontal human figure passing through the trunk of a leafy green tree against a background of blue hills and sky.

Continuation of the discourse on the spiritus vitalis and Mumia (nails, hair, sweat, urine, blood), followed by a first-person account of curing a preacher's severe hand pain in Graz using sympathetic powder on a bloodied cloth, and a rubricated section detailing strict hygienic and ritual rules for handling the bloodied cloths (avoiding menstrual contamination, dust, cold, garlic, onions, and pork).

## Page 508 — IIIF Canvas #453

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0453.JP2` (5758 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0453.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0453.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,250

Illustrated recipes for 'Cichori Fluët' (a blood-purifying chicory and germander extract) and 'Puluis Simpateticus' (sympathetic powder), including two colored illustrations (botanical plants and an emblematic solar/Leo scene) and detailed instructions for preparing the powder during August when the Sun is in Leo, plus its application to stop severe bleeding.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Cichori Fluët. Ein herliche Reinigung des geblüets.** (`#xywh=0,0,5758,6853`): Colored botanical illustration in the upper-left corner of the left column depicting two plants growing from a grassy hillock: a smaller plant with blue flowers (chicory) on the left and a taller woody shrub or tree with red blossoms/leaves on the right.

**Puluis Simpateticus, Wie es gemacht Und Gebraucht Wirdt.** (`#xywh=0,0,5758,6853`): Colored emblematic scene spanning the left column, framed by blue horizontal borders. In the sky, a golden sun shines yellow rays diagonally down onto a banded cylindrical vessel resting on a stone pedestal on the left, while a golden lion (Leo) leaps among clouds at upper left. Below, in a grassy landscape with distant hills, a seated figure in a green doublet and red breeches extends a thin wand or thread toward the vessel.

## Page 509 — IIIF Canvas #454

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0454.JP2` (6005 × 6853 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0454.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0454.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,350

Sympathetic remedies using vitriol/powder (♃) and blood/urine (□) for wounds, fevers, excessive menstruation, and burns, followed by a defense of natural magic and sympathetic powder against accusations of sorcery, recounting a 1696 dinner conversation with Jesuit Father Höpel at Herberstein's estate in Laboth.
